# Phone feature matrix

Latest published **0.1.6 / 741b3718** improves feature search, challenge selection, automation cards and protection setup. [APK and checksum](https://github.com/omeyerrato/SleepWell/releases/tag/v0.1.6-clearer-settings-20261029-741b3718); [package audit](verification/2026-10-29-016-build/README.md); [combined editor action/persistence proof](verification/2026-10-29-editor-usability-integration/README.md). The phone remains on0.1.4 with original user data preserved; installation is deferred by the user. All36-reference acceptance remains unfinished.

Updated 2026-10-29 incrementally against the supplied brief, all 12 previously inspected boards,
SCREEN-MAP.md, current code and official Sleep as Android manual. Screen numbers
are intended reference destinations, not a claim of completed screens.

Feature baseline **170b0e44** includes real backup tone/volume, corrected rails,
Gentle's quiet sample, clearer Tone/QR/Nap/rhythm layouts and the scoped
Health/Privacy/document/Cloud repairs. Original-signer arm64 build passed in
1m22s; remote tag and both uploaded hashes are verified. Selected native flows
exercise actions, cancellation, recreation and persistence. [Exact release scope](verification/2026-10-27-reference-menus-build/README.md).

The last verified phone installation is **0.1.4 / 08d3911e**, with original records, user settings and user files preserved. No physical output test ran. All36 reference states and the broader interaction/security review remain open.

## Current top priorities

- [ ] Match all36 reference screens. Current Report, Tone/Gentle, Snooze, QR, sleep plan, rhythm/Nap, Settings root and Appearance have bounded comparison/action evidence. Remaining screens, variants and full reference fidelity remain open. [Screen-by-screen acceptance](UI-REFERENCE-ACCEPTANCE.md).
- [x] Complete Report calendar/edit verification. Current selected Report method passes55.975s/11 inspected originals, including real calendar/day changes, notes/tags/awake edits, Save/reopen and Back-discard. [Evidence](verification/2026-10-27-report-proportions/README.md).
- [ ] Remaining interaction bugs and security review. Concrete shortcut/touch/calendar/QR/NFC/Health/Privacy/document/Cloud fixes are merged; their selected native scopes pass. Readiness recovery and additional Tracking work are a separate source increment awaiting native acceptance. Broader interaction, provider, hostile-overlay and full security acceptance remain open.

The installed phone is170b0e44; its checksum and data preservation are verified. The0.1.4 update is published and awaiting USB reconnection.

**Implemented**: narrower stated behavior exists; validation is separately listed.
**Partial**: subfeatures still missing. **Missing**: no working implementation.
**Blocked**: stated OS or external prerequisite. Passing compilation, a screenshot,
or a test source file alone is not evidence of functional completion.

Watch companions, fitness bands, oximeters and hardware vital measurements are
excluded. Sonar, optional smart lights and sleep-only Health Connect remain in scope.
Vendor proprietary assets/algorithms and hosted services are not copied.
This matrix supersedes the wearable roadmap in the older FEATURES.md.

Earlier source **f0c1a274** builds app/UI-check APKs in18s, with original signer,
sleep schema17 and backup13. The grouped settings/search/feedback increment passes
one silent organization flow at test-only successor 957f3380, with ten inspected
actual captures. Physical haptics, all destinations and all36 reference screens
remain unverified. [Scoped evidence](verification/2026-10-26-settings-organization/README.md).
Final packaged source **b5c63ad1** builds production in16s (full runtime MIT notice/credit); functional ancestor **731e9725** built in1m5s with radio mixer, grouped Tracking setup, measured echo-rhythm review and explicit experimental recorded-audio inference. Original signer and packaged model hashes match; new Android flows remain unverified. See FEATURE-CHECKLIST.md.
Installed phone source remains **2cbfe043**, original data preserved; no subsequent
phone actions ran. [Phone evidence](verification/2026-10-26-phone-2cbfe043/README.md).
Earlier Report, integration and acoustic evidence remain separately scoped; API
client/delivery, real audio and overnight acceptance are not established by this
settings flow. The last full-app native run remains failing; a narrow pass does
not establish full-app acceptance.

New production behavior includes extra-loud session processing, Smart Bedtime,
reversible awake estimates, Google sleep-confidence input, additional camera/motion/
pitch/game challenges, radio, paired movement, recorded scoring, periodic-echo
experiments, timed dream cues, PIR input, silent guidance/binaural generation,
nap profiles and portable definitions, measured motion calibration, reviewed-day
SRI, relative/speech alarm entry, per-alarm reminder/date/snooze controls, diary
import, standard alarm/timer requests, explicit summary sharing, and separately
consented manual/automatic whole-library audio backups. Exact behavior and limits
are recorded per row and in [remaining work](REMAINING-WORK.md).

At the user's request, the dedicated design reviewer is stopped. Broad design,
bug and security audits are deferred. Real acoustic accuracy, overnight reliability,
provider/hardware exchange and full36screen visual parity remain uncertified.

Recurring starts force microphone/clips/light/sleep sounds off, never open an Activity, never backfill missed starts, and retain4h/12h limits. Protected reconciliation and visible reopening schedule future occurrences only. Force-stop/OEM/permission restrictions and background specialUse distribution review remain; see RECURRING-MOVEMENT.md. Smart-window lead-in now uses the actual projected dated/sunrise/skipped/calendar-adjusted unconsumed deadline; expired or unsupported nap occurrences cannot admit capture. Optional charging/screen-off gates apply only to explicit Arm tonight, not background recurring tokens. Optional Google Sleep API confidence input1270922 adds permission, real registration/removal and current-arm fresh confidence final gate; published21.4.0 dependency compiles; real Google delivery remains unverified. Existing stillness conditions remain required; callback confidence is an estimate, not a sleep stage. Separate default-off false-start rejection is frozen for automatic STILLNESS runs only. Within45min, USER_PRESENT or focused tracking interaction must be followed by2min unlocked interactive use with fresh sustained upright/movement readings; SCREEN_ON alone does not qualify. Pause/alarm/ownership changes suppress it. Actual orderly interrupted stop drains mic/awake work, then transactionally ends and may delete only this newly created untouched local movement record with matching identity and no review/edit/import/link uncertainty. Otherwise the record remains for review. Only its pending audio deletion journal is recovered; unsuccessful unlink stays pending. Both retained/discarded false-start paths skip successful-night backup, never re-arm, and report the outcome. Phone use is a heuristic, not measured wake/sleep; actual false-positive and cleanup behavior remains unverified. This28October source is included in published0.1.5; native captures predate the imagegen raster assets. Full physical/reference acceptance remains open. |
